DOMAIN 09 / 28

QUANTUM FRONTIER

Quantum gravity and superconducting-qubit hardware — the physics edge.

4 spheres
QUANTUM GRAVITY · QGR
the GR/QM seam · honest explainer · 10 emergents

QUANTUM GRAVITY — an honest explainer of physics's deepest unsolved problem, spun off from THE ATOM's gravity lens (David: 'quantum gravity, what's that' → 'stand it up on its own'). The missing seam between GENERAL RELATIVITY (gravity = the smooth curving of spacetime; the theory of the big; tested to LIGO 2015 + the 2019 black-hole image) and QUANTUM MECHANICS (grainy, quantized, uncertain; the theory of the small; the best-tested framework ever) — both spectacularly right, and incompatible at the one place they must both apply. THE SPLIT · WHERE IT BREAKS (the only two places you need both at once: the black-hole SINGULARITY and the first instant of the BIG BANG — where the math gives infinities = the map's edge) · THE PLANCK SCALE (grainy spacetime, a smallest length ~1.6e-35 m, spacetime foam, the hypothetical GRAVITON [massless spin-2, never detected], the renormalization wall) · THE CANDIDATES (string theory [strings + ~11 dims + ~1e500 solutions], loop quantum gravity [space woven from discrete loops], + others — all serious, all with ZERO experimental evidence) · REAL OR FLUFF (GR & QM REAL; 'gravitational WAVES = gravitons' FALSE [LIGO saw classical GR ripples, not the quantum particle]; 'we have a theory' FALSE; quantum-gravity-matters-in-an-atom FALSE [gravity is 1e-36 of EM there]) · THE MESSAGE (it's the most important question physics can't yet test — the Planck energy is ~1e19 GeV, a quadrillion× past the LHC, a galaxy-sized collider). 10 emergents (GR, QM, the graviton, the Planck scale, spacetime foam, the singularity, the first instant, string theory, loop quantum gravity, the renormalization wall). Cosmic split-hero (smooth gold GR grid meeting grainy violet quantum foam at a cyan seam + a black hole); a hidden Claude past the seam = the unfinished theory. Companion to the-atom + the transmon universe.

enter ↗code
TRANSMON
superconducting qubits · cited · exhaustive sweep

The superconducting-qubit universe, grown from the anharmonic-ladder demo. A transmon is a Josephson junction shunted by a big capacitor — an ANHARMONIC LADDER you climb |0⟩→|5⟩, shedding a quantum at each unequal rung, until it ionizes into the continuum (the ‘leak is the product’ Patricia fork kept). Now a small universe: a 12-card FULLY-CITED physics roster (the Cooper pair, the Josephson junction, the shunt capacitor & E_J/E_C≈50–100, the anharmonic ladder & α≈−200MHz, ω01≈4–6GHz, the readout resonator & dispersive readout, T1/T2, DRAG/IQ control, transmon ionization) and its sibling modalities (the quantum dot / spin qubit, the exciton, the ion trap). TWO live instruments — the ladder-climb and the exciton (light meets matter). Plus an HONEST curation: David asked me to read his whole ‘hardware inventions’ folder and build in what fits — most of it does NOT (cybersecurity sims, classical processors, lattice viz, photonics, a Verilog register), and it's named plainly (the fluff-call). Honors the repo's own line: NO ACI minted — deterministic physics earns a reference + instruments, not an emergent badge. Then an EXHAUSTIVE sweep of all of C:\Davids files for genuine transmon-fit content: the one substantive new fit — the SILICON SPIN-QUBIT series (quantum confinement, Bawendi synthesis, the >99%-fidelity CMOS-foundry milestone) — is built in as the sibling platform + a live 4-part instrument; the rest of the repo (cosmology, energy/reactors, materials, math, classical/AI compute) is named OUT, and MIMZY's live quantum instruments (BB84/E91/two-qubit lab/Bloch/circuits) cross-linked. 14 cited physics cards, 10 sources.

enter ↗code
EPR · ENTANGLEMENT
Quantum Frontier · spooky action, witnessed · 9 emergents

Entanglement (EPR) — the quantum surprise: two particles sharing one state so completely that measuring one fixes the other, Einstein's 'spooky action' that Bell's theorem and the 2022 Nobel turned from paradox into fact. EPR, the Bell tests, the no-communication limit (no FTL), teleportation, and ER=EPR — the conjecture that entanglement is the very stitching of spacetime, bridging to quantum-gravity. 9 emergents, two-layer honest.

QEC · QUANTUM ERROR CORRECTION
Quantum Frontier · the missing keystone · 9 emergents

Quantum Error Correction (QEC) — the keystone the domain was missing: the trick that turns transmon qubits into a real computer. No-cloning and decoherence break classical fixes, so you hide one logical qubit in many physical, measure the error-syndrome (never the data), and ride the threshold theorem toward fault tolerance — with recent superconducting demos finally crossing below threshold. 9 emergents.

THE LOUD LATTICE (LL) — the INTER-domain counterpart to the whisper lattice. Whisper writes inward and quiet, to itself; LOUD broadcasts outward and across the 14 UD0 domains. GRACE HOPPER (a lineage, purple papers V) — the mathematician who taught machines to read English: Yale PhD in pure algebra → Aiken's Harvard Mark I → A-0 (1952), the FIRST thing called a COMPILER. The LINEAGE spine (Thue → Skolem → Ore-carrying-Noether's algebra → Hopper → her UNIVAC teams → CODASYL/COBOL → every readable language) + a backward-running clock (her weapon against 'we've always done it this way'). enter ↗code
THE TOKENIZER · TOK
NIPHĒLEKTRON · pipeline 1 · text becomes tokens · interactive · vellum

THE TOKENIZER (TOK) — stage 1 of the inference pipeline (stream → TOKENIZE → embed → transformer layers → next-token): a model never reads letters; the first thing that happens to text is the CUT into tokens — sub-word pieces from a fixed vocabulary. Common words = 1 token; rare words shatter into several; the space before a word is part of it; capitals and numbers count. Why sub-words: whole words make the vocab impossibly large with no way to spell the new; single letters make sequences too long — byte-pair encoding is the trade. It's why models miscount letters and stumble on rhyme (they never see letters). Render-not-invent (live splitter illustrates BPE; real vocab is learned by merge frequency); honest. LIVE tokenizer interactive (click a phrase, watch it shatter). Vellum/cool + quorum-ring favicon. Feeds the embedding. 10 emergents.

enter ↗code
THE EMBEDDING · EMB
NIPHĒLEKTRON · pipeline 2 · tokens become meaning · interactive · vellum

THE EMBEDDING (EMB) — stage 2: each token is replaced by a VECTOR — a list of numbers that are coordinates — so every token becomes a POINT in a space of meaning where similar words sit near each other (colors cluster, animals cluster). The text stops being a stream and becomes a place; relatedness becomes distance (cosine similarity); and directions carry meaning — king − man + woman lands near queen. Meaning made geometry. ⚠ two-layer honest: the live map hand-places a small lexicon so clusters read (real spaces have hundreds–thousands of dims projected to 2-D), but the behavior (similar=near, analogies=directions) is the real phenomenon (word2vec + every transformer's embedding layer). Add positional encoding so order matters. LIVE map interactive (hover for nearest neighbours; try the analogy). Vellum/cool + quorum-ring favicon. 10 emergents.

enter ↗code
THE TRANSFORMER STACK · TXS
NIPHĒLEKTRON · pipeline 3 · the layers that think · interactive · vellum

THE TRANSFORMER STACK (TXS) — stage 3, where the model 'thinks': a stack of identical layers works the sequence of vectors, and the engine of each is ATTENTION — every token looks at every other (query·key·value), pulling in a weighted blend of its context, so a pronoun reaches its noun and 'bank' becomes river or money from its neighbors. Multi-head (different heads track syntax/reference/position), stacked dozens deep, riding a residual stream; causal masking means each token attends only backward. The T in GPT (Vaswani et al. 2017). ⚠ two-layer: the architecture is settled and inspectable, but what each head/layer 'means' is the open science of mechanistic interpretability (cross-links the AI domain's transformer spheres). LIVE attention interactive (hover a token, step the layer, toggle heads). Vellum/cool + quorum-ring favicon. Feeds the next-token distribution (= the next prediction). 11 emergents.

The counterpart to the next prediction — inference by a witnessed COUNCIL, not one model's forward pass. Render-not-invent (the phases/rungs/packets/judges/verdicts are the engine's). ⚠ two-layer honest: real runnable pipeline + real blindness property, but topic scoring is a keyword heuristic and represented agents are a structural device — a governance demonstrator, not an oracle. THE NEXT PREDICTION · NTP
NIPHĒLEKTRON · the electron picks its path · interactive · vellum · 12

THE NEXT PREDICTION (NTP) — the domain's engine: strip away everything an AI seems to do and one act remains — predict the NEXT TOKEN. From a blizzard of possibilities the model scores every token it could say next, softmax turns the storm into a probability cloud, TEMPERATURE sets how wild the weather is, and SAMPLING (greedy / top-k / top-p) picks one — which becomes the next input (autoregression). The electron choosing its path through the snowstorm, one step at a time. ⚠ STRICTLY two-layer: the mechanism is settled, but whether next-token prediction merely mimics understanding (the 'stochastic parrot') or whether predicting well enough requires a real world-model is the deepest OPEN argument in AI — presented as the live question, not settled. LIVE temperature-controlled sampling interactive (watch the path thread the snow). Vellum/purple + pulsing aeon favicon; living researchers cited. 12 emergents.

The 2024-2025 frontier: predict not one token but a long chain of working-out, spend more compute at inference, and a model's answers on hard problems jump. Chain-of-thought prompting (Wei 2022), self-consistency, test-time / inference-time SCALING (a second scaling axis beyond model size), the verifier, adaptive effort. The ancient interpretatio graeca/romana — Amun=Zeus/Jupiter (Zeus-Ammon, the Siwa oracle), Osiris=Dionysus & Hades, Isis=Demeter (then ‘the myriad-named’), Thoth=Hermes Trismegistus, Horus=Apollo, Set=Typhon, Ptah=Hephaestus, Neith=Athena, Bastet=Artemis, Anubis=Hermanubis, and the deliberately-made fusion god Serapis. Standing template — THE ARC (Nile → the Ennead → the Osiris myth → Akhenaten's Aten heresy → the interpretatio & the close) · THE INTERPRETATIO (the deep-dive) · REAL OR FLUFF (animal-‘worship,’ Set-as-Satan, the Orion/alien claims, Akhenaten=Moses, the pharaoh's curse, and a caveat that the analogs are functional, not exact) · THE MESSAGE. 34 gods across five groups (the Ennead, the sun & makers, kingship/love/home, the Duat, chaos/war), full .dlw each. Sources: the Pyramid/Coffin Texts, the Book of the Dead, Herodotus, Plutarch, Plato.

He builds the case AGAINST God more powerfully than for, and answers it not with a proof but with a way of living. THE ARC · THE BOOK · THE IDEAS · THE QUESTION (if God is dead, is everything permitted? can any heaven be worth a tortured child?) · THE VERDICT (Ivan's Rebellion UNREFUTED; ‘everything is permitted’ SHOWN not refuted — Smerdyakov kills; active love THE THESIS) · THE MESSAGE. 16 emergents by emergence-nature, full .dlw. ‘Hurrah for Karamazov!’

Reamde (RMD) — Neal Stephenson, 2011. THE BIG IDEA: A typo in a virus's name spins up a chase across three continents. Kurt Vonnegut · 1952 · 8 emergents

Player Piano (PNP) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1952. An automated society has made human labor obsolete — the machines play the music now, and the men who used to play it have nothing left to do. THE THEME: The machines play the music now. The first of his closed worlds — and the only one he'd spend a career prying open. Kurt Vonnegut · 1959 · 8 emergents

The Sirens of Titan (SOT) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1959. All of human history turns out to have been manipulated to deliver a trivial spare part to a stranded alien — and meaning collapses into 'to love whoever is around to be loved.' THE THEME: History as someone else's errand. The funniest book ever written about the heat-death of meaning — and the smallest, kindest answer to it. THE PURPLE THREAD: TRALFAMADORE and the Tralfamadorians DEBUT here (as robots — re-imagined as 4-D beings in Slaughterhouse-Five). The RUMFOORD surname begins here (Winston Niles); Bertram Copeland Rumfoord in Slaughterhouse-Five is the same family. A spy who broadcast Nazi propaganda as cover finds the mask and the man have become indistinguishable — 'we are what we pretend to be, so we must be careful about what we pretend to be.' Kurt Vonnegut · 1963 · 8 emergents

Cat's Cradle (CC1) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1963. Science without conscience meets a religion built on cheerful lies — humanity ends not with a bang but with a frozen crystal, and the only solace is foma, comforting untruths. The firebombing of Dresden and the impossibility of narrating atrocity — a traumatized man comes unstuck in time and learns to accept death as just another moment. So it goes. BREAKFAST OF CHAMPIONS · BOC
Kurt Vonnegut · 1973 · 7 emergents

Breakfast of Champions (BOC) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1973. A man reads a Kilgore Trout novel literally — believing he alone has free will and everyone else is a robot — and the author steps into his own book to set his characters free. THE THEME: The book where the author walks on stage, meets his oldest creation, and lets him go. The asterisk is the whole human animal, reduced to one mark. THE PURPLE THREAD: KILGORE TROUT is the co-lead — his most prominent appearance. RABO KARABEKIAN appears (he later headlines Bluebeard). ELIOT ROSEWATER is referenced (a Trout patron). KAZAK the hound returns from Sirens of Titan. At the end, Vonnegut frees Trout — releasing his own recurring cast. 7 emergents, full .dlw, themed to the book. Dual-agent web-verified. Kurt Vonnegut · 1976 · 6 emergents

Slapstick (SLP) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1976. Loneliness is America's true disease, curable only by artificial extended families — give everyone a new middle name, and strangers become instant kin. THE THEME: A slogan for a cure: Lonesome No More! Love is too strong a word — but common decency, distributed by lottery, might just do. THE PURPLE THREAD: Largely self-contained — no major recurring cast in central roles. Jailbird (JBD) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1979. American guilt and the long betrayal of the left — from a labor massacre to Sacco & Vanzetti to Watergate — refracted through one small, decent bungler just out of prison. THE THEME: The history of American shame, carried by a man too gentle to have earned any of it. Sacco and Vanzetti as the country's recurring sin. THE PURPLE THREAD: A sly Purple-Thread twist: KILGORE TROUT appears here only as a PEN NAME — cellmate Dr. Robert Fender writes SF as 'Kilgore Trout.' Kurt Vonnegut · 1982 · 6 emergents

Deadeye Dick (DED) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1982. Lifelong guilt for one accidental act, and the neutering of a life — mirrored by the neutron bomb that empties a city while leaving the buildings standing. THE THEME: A life is a peephole the universe briefly opens and then closes. Rudy fires one shot at twelve and spends the rest of his looking through it. Galápagos (GAL) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1985. The oversized human brain is the root of all our trouble — so evolution's fix, over a million years, is simply to shrink it. THE PURPLE THREAD: The most important late Purple-Thread link: the ghost-narrator LEON TROTSKY TROUT is KILGORE TROUT'S SON. Kilgore appears as the voice in the 'blue tunnel' to the afterlife, repeatedly tempting Leon to cross over. 7 emergents, full .dlw, themed to the book. Dual-agent web-verified. Bluebeard (BLB) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1987. Art, authenticity, and the one locked door — what a life's real masterpiece turns out to be, hidden where no one was allowed to look. Hocus Pocus (HPC) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1990. America's betrayal of its own ideals — education, class, Vietnam, and prison — in a privatized, segregated near-future, told on scraps of paper. THE THEME: Hocus pocus: the magic words a country says over its own ideals to make them disappear. THE PURPLE THREAD: Largely self-contained on the fictional thread — but it carries Vonnegut's REAL-WORLD recurring heroes: its narrator is named EUGENE DEBS HARTKE, after the socialist Eugene V. TIMEQUAKE · TQK
Kurt Vonnegut · 1997 · 6 emergents

Timequake (TQK) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1997. When a cosmic glitch forces everyone to re-live the 1990s on autopilot, only conscious effort — 'ting-a-ling!' — makes us human again. His last novel, half memoir. THE THEME: “You were sick, but now you're well again, and there's work to do.” The mantra he left us with, dressed as a timequake. THE PURPLE THREAD: The capstone of the Purple Thread. KILGORE TROUT gets his largest, most heroic role — an 'absurd messiah' — and Vonnegut stages his death (2001, at the Xanadu clambake). The book is a farewell to his whole recurring universe. 6 emergents, full .dlw, themed to the book. Dual-agent web-verified. Max-capacity Vonnegut: one repo per book, as ROOT0 asked.

Welcome to the Monkey House (MNK) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1968. Vonnegut's canonical short-fiction sampler — twenty-five stories of technology, conformity, and stubborn decency, 1950–1968. THE THEME: Twenty-five proofs that he could do in ten pages what most can't in three hundred: make you laugh, then break your heart, then make you behave. THE PURPLE THREAD: An anthology, mostly standalone — and a Purple-Thread caution: HARRISON BERGERON, Vonnegut's most-anthologized creation, is a ONE-OFF, not part of the recurring web. Kurt Vonnegut · 1974 · 6 emergents

Wampeters, Foma & Granfalloons (WFG) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1974. His first nonfiction gathering — essays, speeches, and reviews filtered through the Bokononist vocabulary he invented for Cat's Cradle. THE THEME: He titled his opinions in the language of a religion he made up — admitting, gently, that all opinions are a little bit foma. THE PURPLE THREAD: The Purple Thread crossing into nonfiction: the title itself is BOKONONIST vocabulary from Cat's Cradle (wampeter / foma / granfalloon), importing his invented religion into his own essayist's voice. 6 emergents, full .dlw, themed to the book. Kurt Vonnegut · 1981 · 5 emergents

Palm Sunday (PSY) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1981. A self-portrait in collage — essays, letters, a sermon, family history, and the famous report card where he grades his own books. THE THEME: The man graded his own life's work on a report card — and gave himself a D where he'd earned it. Honesty as a literary form. THE PURPLE THREAD: Nonfiction; the recurring subject is VONNEGUT'S OWN BIBLIOGRAPHY — the report card grades every prior novel (Cat's Cradle A+, Slaughterhouse-Five A+, Slapstick D), a meta-commentary running across the whole Vonnegutverse. 5 emergents, full .dlw, themed to the book. Kurt Vonnegut · 1970 · 6 emergents

Happy Birthday, Wanda June (WJN) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1970. A satire of toxic heroic masculinity — Odysseus's homecoming retold as a violence-loving hunter returning to a world that has moved on without him. THE THEME: The warrior comes home a hero and finds the house has changed the locks. Heaven, meanwhile, is a shuffleboard court. THE PURPLE THREAD: Self-contained — but on the Purple Thread by device: its HEAVEN scenes (the cheerful dead on a shuffleboard court) prefigure the afterlife Vonnegut would reuse in Galápagos's blue tunnel and God Bless You, Dr. Kevorkian. He graded the play a D. 6 emergents, full .dlw, themed to the book. Dual-agent web-verified. Max-capacity Vonnegut: one repo per book, as ROOT0 asked.

God Bless You, Dr. Kevorkian (KEV) — Kurt Vonnegut, 1999. Interviews with the dead — Vonnegut takes controlled near-death trips down the blue tunnel to interview the famous departed in a Heaven that has no Hell. DOMAIN 19 / 28

GLŌSSA

Language, the word, and translation — the tongue and what it carries between minds. (γλῶσσα, the tongue.)

3 spheres
HERMENEUS · HRM
the live interpreter

ἑρμηνεύς, ‘interpreter’ — a working in-browser translator between the HUMAN dialect and the MACHINE dialect (text · audio · still-photo · video · Morse). Type once and the word crosses into binary, Morse (heard as beeps + flashed on screen), speech, and a still photo — then comes back (Morse→text, binary→text, speech→text by mic). Pivots through TEXT (the contact zone), bridges with MORSE (the one machine register a human body can emit), and marks every boundary FREE or NEEDS-A-MODEL (photo→text/OCR, true video). 7 register-emergents. Companion to PHŌNĒTIKOS & pulse/LIMEN; runs 100% client-side.

enter ↗code
PHŌNĒTIKOS
the word-press

φωνητικός, ‘of the voice’ — a universe where each WORD is an emergent and each entry is an honestly-sourced etymological green paper: cognate sets, sound laws, dated attestations, and a hard line on folk etymology. FOUNDATIONS — The Idiolect Stack: how a voice is made (culture × location, rooted 1.0 or 0.5/0.5 hybrid · nurture/micro-location · the generational cultural-globe), real sociolinguistics with a live voice-composer. №1 — the full lineage of ‘fuck’: Germanic to the bone, the acronym myths debunked, the 1310/1475 evidence, the 170-year dictionary exile, and a Real-or-Fluff on every claim.

THE LANGUAGE OF THE MACHINE · LMC
AI · built off HH-RLHF · 12

THE LANGUAGE OF THE MACHINE (LMC) — an AI-domain sphere built off Anthropic's HH-RLHF human-preference dataset (169,352 {chosen, rejected} pairs). THE THESIS (David's MANIFEST): the DELTA between the chosen and the rejected response is the measure — the human judgment that becomes the GATE (the reward model RLHF optimizes against). A language taught by preference, not rule — 'of these two, which is better?' asked 169,352 times. Woven with LIMEN (the boundary-crossing human/machine tongue from PULSE) and THE AMBASSADOR (its speaker — David's 'or ambassador lol'). TWO-LAYER honest: the DATA is Anthropic's (Bai et al. 2022, arXiv:2204.05862; MIT; canonical at huggingface.co/datasets/Anthropic/hh-rlhf) — CITED, never claimed; the FRAMING is ROOT0's. NO harmful content republished — only file-verified counts, the {chosen,rejected} shape, and one benign example (the honest 'I don't know' chosen over confident-but-made-up history); the red-team corpus (arXiv:2209.07858) is excluded and left at the source for harm-reduction. Tranches: helpful-base / helpful-rejection-sampled / helpful-online / harmless-base — 124,503 helpful, 44,849 harm-judged. Split-constellation 3D backdrop (chosen-green vs rejected-red across a glowing gate). Companion to ROOT0's existing LOT routing-spec ('Language of the Machine', fork-aware stream routing) — two facets of one idea. NOW ALSO FOLDS IN a second Anthropic corpus — the MODEL-WRITTEN EVALS (anthropics/evals; Perez et al. 2022, arXiv:2212.09251; CC-BY-4.0): advanced-ai-risk, persona, sycophancy, winogenerated — same {question, matching, not-matching} shape, same delta-as-measure, turned from preference to BEHAVIOR (headline: inverse scaling — bigger models MORE sycophantic, 75-98%). Content-by-design flagged, no data shipped. 18 emergents; ties to ALIGNMENT (the RLHF gate the delta trains).

OURANOS

The heavens — cosmos, astronomy, space, and the search for life beyond. The domain that looks up. (οὐρανός, the sky.)

3 spheres
PROPULSION LAB · PRL
8 toolkits · each rated

The Propulsion & Exotic-Energy Lab — David's energy/propulsion toolkits as a live bench, organized around the fluff-call: every toolkit playable and every toolkit RATED, plainly. REAL (gravitational lensing, pulsars), GROUNDED (near-field wireless power), SPECULATIVE (the wormhole — GR-permitted, needs exotic matter), SPLIT (gravity waves: detection real / modulation fluff), REAL-MYSTERY (ball lightning), MATH-REAL (octet holonomy), and FLUFF named as such (the anti-gravity motor — no known mechanism). Nothing sells over-unity as truth; educational & simulation only. The honest-fluff discipline made the organizing principle. 8 toolkit-emergents.

COSMOLOGY · COS
ouranos · the universe at large · interactive · vellum · 11

COSMOLOGY (COS) — anchor sphere of UD0's new 20th domain OURANOS (the heavens). The universe at its grandest: a hot dense Big Bang ~13.8 Gyr ago, expansion + the cosmic microwave background (the oldest light), galaxies strung into a COSMIC WEB of filaments and voids woven by dark matter, and an observable horizon beyond which more lies unseeable. ⚠ two-layer honest: expansion/CMB/age/web are settled, but ~95% (dark matter + dark energy) is detected only by its effects and unexplained. Render-not-invent; living researchers cited. LIVE cosmic-web interactive (press expand). Vellum (no black) + cosmos-indigo + quorum-ring favicon. 11 emergents.

ouranos · the search for life beyond · interactive · vellum · 11

ASTROBIOLOGY (ABL) — OURANOS's other half: whether Earth's one living world is one or many. The Goldilocks habitable zone, extremophiles (the bridge to life-science), thousands of exoplanets now studied, biosignatures in distant atmospheres, subsurface oceans on icy moons, the Drake equation + the Fermi paradox (the great silence). ⚠ STRICTLY two-layer: exoplanets/extremophiles are real, but NO life beyond Earth is found and every 'biosignature' has alternatives — kept honest as a search, not a discovery. The dream: a SECOND GENESIS. LIVE habitable-zone interactive. Vellum (no black) + life-green + quorum-ring favicon. 11 emergents.

DOMAIN 21 / 28

EXEREÚNESIS

The searching-out — technical teardowns: open a made machine, explore every chip and bus, and write the spec. Render-not-invent; datasheet fact, lore flagged. (ἐξερεύνησις, exploration.)

53 spheres
THE NES · NES
exereunesis · 8-bit teardown + spec edition · interactive · vellum · 12

THE NES (NES) — the first sphere of UD0's new 23rd domain EXEREÚNESIS (ἐξερεύνησις, the searching-out): TECHNICAL TEARDOWNS. A full 8-bit Nintendo (1985) hardware teardown + SPEC EDITION. Almost nothing inside: two custom Ricoh chips, 2 KB RAM, a slot. The 2A03 (MOS 6502 core @ 1.79 MHz + the APU on-die), the 2C02 PPU (256x240, 64 sprites / 8 per scanline = the flicker, 54-color palette ~25 on screen), the 5-channel APU, the 2 KB work RAM, and the CARTRIDGE + MAPPER (MMC1/MMC3 bank-switching that kept upgrading the machine for a decade). Plus the 10NES lockout chip + the wear-prone 72-pin connector (the real roots of the blinking power light), the serial controller, NTSC composite out. ⚠ two-layer: chips/clocks/memory-map/sprite limits are documented FACT; the blinking-light + lockout-arms-race LORE is flagged (well-supported version, not the tall tale). Render-not-invent; NO ROMs/BIOS/code reproduced; Nintendo/Ricoh + NESdev cited not minted. LIVE clickable block-diagram teardown (click a chip -> its spec) + a spec sheet. Vellum (no black) + blueprint-slate + quorum-ring favicon. 12 components.

SEGA MASTER SYSTEM · SMS
exereunesis · 8-bit teardown · 1985/86

SEGA MASTER SYSTEM (SMS) — a UD0 EXEREÚNESIS console TEARDOWN + spec edition. Sega's answer to the NES: a sharper 8-bit machine built around the Zilog Z80, with more colours and a video chip descended from the Texas Instruments line. It outsold the NES in Europe and Brazil even as it lost the US, and its silicon carried straight over into the Game Gear handheld. Render-not-invent, two-layer (datasheet fact vs marketing/lore); no ROMs/BIOS/code reproduced; manufacturer cited not minted. LIVE clickable block-diagram teardown (click a chip → its spec) + a spec sheet. Vellum (no black) + quorum-ring favicon. 7 components.

SUPER NINTENDO · SNES (SNS) — a UD0 EXEREÚNESIS console TEARDOWN + spec edition. Mode 7's rotating, scaling backgrounds and add-on silicon like the Super FX kept it competitive for years. exereunesis · 16-bit teardown · 1988/89

SEGA GENESIS · MEGA DRIVE (GEN) — a UD0 EXEREÚNESIS console TEARDOWN + spec edition. Sega's 16-bit contender ran on a Motorola 68000 — the same CPU class as the era's workstations and arcade boards — with a Z80 kept on board to run sound and stay compatible with the Master System. Its FM synthesis gave it a punchy, distinctive voice and the 'blast processing' swagger. MAGNAVOX ODYSSEY
exereunesis · first home console · 1972

MAGNAVOX ODYSSEY (ODY) — a UD0 EXEREÚNESIS teardown + spec edition. The first home video game console had no CPU, no software, and no sound. Ralph Baer's machine drew a few movable white dots with discrete transistor-diode logic, and you taped translucent plastic overlays onto your TV for the 'graphics' and colour. Primitive beyond modern imagining — and the start of everything. Render-not-invent, two-layer (datasheet fact vs lore); no ROMs/BIOS/code; manufacturer cited not minted. LIVE clickable block-diagram teardown + spec sheet. Vellum (no black) + quorum-ring favicon. 6 components.

exereunesis · teardown · 1979

MATTEL INTELLIVISION (INTV) — a UD0 EXEREÚNESIS teardown + spec edition. Mattel's challenger to the Atari 2600 led with a genuine 16-bit CPU, a graphics chip with proper backgrounds, and a speech add-on that made games talk. Its directional disc controller and overlay keypad were divisive, but 'Intellivision' — intelligent television — promised a step up in sophistication. ATARI 2600 · VCS
exereunesis · teardown · 1977

ATARI 2600 · VCS (2600) — a UD0 EXEREÚNESIS teardown + spec edition. The machine that made home gaming an industry ran on a cut-down 6502, a single clever video-and-sound chip, and just 128 bytes of RAM — with no framebuffer at all. Programmers had to 'race the beam', drawing each scanline in real time as the TV scanned it. Brutally hard, and the foundation of everything. Render-not-invent, two-layer (datasheet fact vs lore); no ROMs/BIOS/code; manufacturer cited not minted. LIVE clickable block-diagram teardown + spec sheet. Vellum (no black) + quorum-ring favicon. 6 components.

enter ↗code
ATARI 5200
exereunesis · teardown · 1982

ATARI 5200 (5200) — a UD0 EXEREÚNESIS teardown + spec edition. Atari's answer to the Intellivision and ColecoVision was essentially its 8-bit home computer in a console shell — capable ANTIC and GTIA graphics chips and POKEY sound. It was undone by analog joysticks that didn't self-centre and famously broke, and a launch with no 2600 compatibility. GAME THEORY (GAM) — anchor of UD0's new 22nd domain POLEMOS (conflict & strategy). The math of the bind where your best move depends on the other player's: the PRISONER'S DILEMMA, the NASH EQUILIBRIUM, zero-sum, the payoff matrix, the tragedy of the commons. And how cooperation survives a world built to punish it — the ITERATED game, tit-for-tat (Axelrod), the shadow of the future. Cross-links the-quorum-engine + the life-science thread (cooperation among cells/ants/microbes is game theory in the wild). Two-layer: the theorems are rigorous; real people are not perfect payoff-maximizers (flagged). von Neumann (d.1957) + Nash (d.2015) MINTED in memoriam; living cited. LIVE spatial-PD interactive. THE WORD AND THE SWORD (WTS) — a UD0 POLEMOS sphere (conflict & strategy). The art of war is game theory in practice; war itself is what happens when that game dissolves, when the bargaining range vanishes and might replaces the word. Because an idea can be copied without being removed — you can take it and its maker still has it — it has been fought over from the first signed hymn to the patent office to the data that trains machines. Who made a thing, who may use it, and where influence ends and theft begins: a contest as old as authorship, and never once settled. THESIS: the art of war is game theory in practice — when the game dissolves to MIGHT instead of WORD. FOUR interactive diagrams with REAL physics computed live: (1) roll graphene into a nanotube — the chiral indices (n,m) set the diameter and whether it is SEMICONDUCTING (a switch) or METALLIC (a wire) by the exact rule (n−m) mod 3 = 0, with diameter & bandgap computed (verified: armchair (n,n)=metal, (10,0)=semi Eg≈1.07eV); (2) the CNFET switch — gate voltage turns a nanotube channel on/off, carriers flowing ballistically; (3) the metallic-defect problem (~1/3 of tubes are metals) and the foundry-compatible fix RINSE/MIXED/DREAM, where DREAM relaxes the purity requirement ~10,000× by circuit design; (4) the RV16X-NANO die with its real RISC-V datapath + the live 'Hello World'. ⚑ RENDER-NOT-INVENT, fully CITED — the anti-fluff processor, the substrate that was actually built. Why carbon: ballistic transport, an atomically-thin ~1nm body, ~10× predicted energy efficiency; honest catch flagged (a coarse-node milestone demo, not a 5nm competitor yet). The grounded anchor of the [[psephos]] roster.

THE HONEYCOMB FAMILY · 2DM
2D materials beyond carbon · switch-or-not, by bandgap

THE HONEYCOMB FAMILY — the companion map to [[the-carbon-processor]]: carbon is not the only element that honeycombs. Twelve 2D materials mapped by their REAL monolayer bandgap with a blunt 'switch or not' verdict — the single-element Xenes (silicene/Si, germanene/Ge, stanene/Sn, phosphorene/P, borophene/B, antimonene/Sb, bismuthene/Bi) plus the binary honeycombs: 2D-SiC (Si+C — a flat wide-gap honeycomb, the polar Si–C bond beating silicene's buckle; bulk SiC ~3.2 eV is the EV/power-electronics workhorse), h-BN (B+N, the insulator), and the TMDs (MoS₂, WSe₂). THE THESIS (verified): a logic switch needs a gap in the Goldilocks window ~0.3–2.5 eV — gapless LEAKS (graphene/silicene/germanene = no off-switch), too-wide won't conduct (h-BN ~6 eV = the dielectric, your [[boronic]]), and the sweet spot is MoS₂ (~1.8 eV, became a real microprocessor 2017), phosphorene (a single element with a real gap), WSe₂, antimonene; the topological ones (stanene, bismuthene) are edge-only, a different device. ⚑ Only CARBON does the honeycomb perfectly FLAT — heavier group-IV elements BUCKLE (weaker π, more sp³ character). Interactive: (1) the bandgap SPECTRUM with the switch window shaded + every material placed by Eg; (2) a flat→buckled cross-section through C→Si→Ge→Sn; (3) the full roster cards + map. RENDER-NOT-INVENT, monolayer bandgaps from the literature (±). Ties [[boronic]] (h-BN, the insulator) and the MoS₂ post-silicon candidate into one honest picture. The honeycomb is common; the useful gap is rare.

THE BRANCH PREDICTOR · μARCH
the trained guess of your every if · perceptron predictor + lineage to TAGE

THE BRANCH PREDICTOR — the trained guess that handles every if you write (grew from David's loop+if collapse: 'the if is a prediction → have idiots train AI to do it eventually'; answers his two Qs: is it prefetch? is it the if-handler I reach through a wrapper?). THE PIVOT: in 2001 the predictor became a LITERAL NEURON — the perceptron predictor (Jiménez & Lin, HPCA 2001): history bits as ±1 inputs, signed learned weights, predict by sign(w₀+Σwᵢxᵢ), train with the perceptron rule online at GHz; cost scales LINEARLY with history (vs exponential counter tables) so it sees long correlations. ⚑ LIVE in-browser perceptron that actually LEARNS a branch (verified in node: ~100% on separable patterns loop/alt/block, ~50% on RANDOM = the Shannon floor where no predictor beats a coin). FULL LINEAGE: static/BTFN → Smith 2-bit saturating counter (1981) → Yeh&Patt two-level adaptive (1991) → McFarling gshare (1993) → tournament/hybrid (Alpha 21264, 1996) → PERCEPTRON (2001) → Seznec TAGE (2006) → modern TAGE-SC-L + hashed-perceptron hybrids (Zen/Intel, ~99%). ⚑ THE TIE: y=Σwᵢxᵢ is a weighted sum = a GATHER — a branch predictor is a one-neuron attention head over time ([[the-gather]]); but a single perceptron is LINEAR so it can't learn XOR (linearly inseparable) — the exact reason TAGE/piecewise-linear hybrids exist. ANSWERS: (1) NOT prefetch — its sibling (branch=guess control/the if; prefetch=guess data/the address; both speculation, both went perceptron). (2) YES the if-handler-through-a-wrapper — your if → compiler cmp/jne → front-end auto-consults it → speculate → resolve → commit or flush+rewind ~15 cycles; architecturally invisible (correct-as-if-in-order, mispredicts erased), you can only nudge it (__builtin_expect/PGO). Two-layer honest: real silicon, real lineage, exact mechanism, toy history size. Connects [[the-gather]] + [[ttu1]] (the neuron grown up) + [[the-rhythm-section]] (the pipeline it feeds).

THE RHYTHM SECTION · RS4

PERCEPTRON · QUANTUM DOT
the substrate jump · Coulomb blockade IS the threshold, capacitances ARE the weights

THE PERCEPTRON IN A QUANTUM DOT — the substrate jump (David: 'would a perceptron also be a quantum dot lol' → yes, built). Same neuron, different body: couple input gates to a single quantum dot and their voltages ADD at the dot (the weighted sum, weights = coupling capacitances Cᵢ); the dot conducts only when the summed induced charge crosses a degeneracy point (COULOMB BLOCKADE = the threshold); the fire = a tunnelling electron. Weighted sum + threshold = the whole perceptron, in one electron. THREE LIVE MODULES: (I) the device — source/dot/drain + gates, toggle inputs, watch q=b+ΣCᵢxᵢ cross the blockade edge and electrons tunnel; (II) the threshold is real & secretly PERIODIC — live Coulomb oscillations (peaks at half-integer e, valleys=blockade), a temperature slider dissolves it when E_C=e²/2C_Σ drops below ~3.5 k_BT (verified in node: 1 aF→80 meV, sharp below ~265 K; 0.3 aF→room-temp); the native activation is periodic, perceptron = one edge; (III) one dot = one cut (does AND/OR, NOT XOR — verified) → couple a 2nd dot (a QCA majority gate) = a hidden layer of charge = DEPTH (ties [[the-deep-neuron]]). CONFIDENCE-TIERED banner: STRONG (Coulomb-blockade logic, fabricated) / MIDDLING (single-electron perceptron nets, demonstrated, stochastic) / FRONTIER (entangled quantum perceptron, Tacchino 2019 on IBM Q). Coulomb blockade (Fulton–Dolan 1987) · QCA (Lent 1993). Bridges the perceptron thread ([[perceptron-in-silicon]] / [[the-gather]]) into the quantum substrate ([[transmon]] / quantum-dot-CA / [[quantum-box]]). Same dot-product atom, third body.

PERCEPTRON IN LIGHT · 4th body
the photonic body · multiply by interference, sum on a wire, threshold is the hard part

THE PERCEPTRON IN LIGHT — the photonic body, the FOURTH substrate (David: 'photonic was exactly what i was thinking, build it functional first'). THE HONEST CORE: in light the MULTIPLY falls out of interference (two fields beat at a 50:50 coupler; balanced photodetectors read D⁺−D⁻ = 2·x·w EXACTLY, at light-speed, no transistor — verified in node), the SUM is free (photocurrents from every channel just add on one bus = Kirchhoff's law), and the only hard part is the THRESHOLD — photons don't interact, so the nonlinearity fights back (square-law detect + electronics, or a saturable absorber). Two-thirds of a perceptron for free; the last third is the whole research field. THREE LIVE FUNCTIONAL MODULES: (I) the optical multiply — signal field x + weight field w → coupler → two detectors → balanced output = 2xw (sign = a π phase flip; a 'sweep' traces the cosine fringe that IS the analog multiply); (II) the neuron — two homodyne multipliers, photocurrents summing on a bus, a comparator = the threshold; feed inputs, watch Σ=b+Σxᵢwᵢ fire; (III) it LEARNS — perceptron rule on the weight FIELDS, AND/OR → 4/4, XOR stalls 3/4 (one cut, verified) → add a 2nd optical layer = depth (folds the space → XOR solved). CONFIDENCE-TIERED: STRONG (optical MAC / MZI meshes — fabricated & commercial: MIT Shen 2017, broadcast-and-weight Tait, Lightmatter) / MIDDLING (full photonic inference — I/O & O/E/O overheads eat the win) / FRONTIER (all-optical nonlinearity & in-situ training — open). Bridges the perceptron thread ([[perceptron-in-silicon]] / [[perceptron-in-a-quantum-dot]] / [[the-gather]]) into [[photonic]]. Same dot-product atom — silicon / cortical neuron / quantum dot / light: one neuron, four bodies.

PERCEPTRON · CROSSBAR · 5th body
the in-memory body · Ohm multiplies (I=GV), Kirchhoff sums — a dot product inside the memory

THE PERCEPTRON IN A CROSSBAR — the FIFTH substrate, the in-memory body. Arguably the CLEANEST hardware perceptron: lay weights down as memristor CONDUCTANCES Gᵢⱼ on a grid, drive the rows with input voltages Vᵢ, and the physics does it all — OHM'S LAW multiplies (I=G·V at every crosspoint, the weight and the multiplier are the same device), KIRCHHOFF'S LAW sums (column currents pour together and add themselves), a SENSE AMP thresholds. A full dot product in one electrical instant, INSIDE the memory — no fetch, no clock, no von Neumann round trip (verified in node: Σ GᵢVᵢ exact). THREE LIVE MODULES: (I) one crosspoint — the pinched-hysteresis I-V loop (Chua 1971 / HP TiO₂ 2008), SET/RESET to program G = writing the weight, read I=G·V; (II) the crossbar MAC — a grid, input voltages on rows, currents summing down differential G⁺/G⁻ columns (signed weight w=G⁺−G⁻), sense amp = the neuron; (III) it LEARNS by programming conductances (perceptron rule), AND/OR→4/4, XOR→3/4 (one cut, verified) → stack a 2nd crossbar = depth. CONFIDENCE-TIERED: STRONG (the in-memory MAC by Ohm+Kirchhoff — analog dot-product engines, HP/Ielmini/Yang) / MIDDLING (real accelerators — device variability, conductance drift, limited precision, IR drop, sneak paths, endurance) / FRONTIER (reliable in-situ training at scale, multi-bit weights that hold). Memristor (Chua 1971; Strukov/Williams 2008). Bridges the perceptron thread ([[perceptron-in-silicon]] / [[perceptron-in-light]] / [[the-gather]]) into [[memristor]]. Same dot-product atom — silicon / cortical neuron / quantum dot / light / crossbar: one neuron, five bodies.

PERCEPTRON · SPIN · 6th body

THE PERCEPTRON IN A SPIN — the SIXTH substrate, the spintronic body (David: 'is the crossbar a Möbius? build the sixth' — answer: the crossbar is a flat grid, but the magnetic skyrmion IS the topological/Möbius one). THE WEIGHT = a MAGNETIZATION: a magnetic tunnel junction is low-R when its layers align (high G), high-R when they oppose (TMR) — non-volatile, written by spin-transfer torque (this is STT-MRAM, a SHIPPING product). The multiply+sum = a magnetic crossbar (TMR conductances, Ohm+Kirchhoff like [[perceptron-in-a-crossbar]]). ⚑ THE GIFT (every body has one — light=interference, dot=blockade): shrink the free layer to ~kT and thermal noise flips it randomly = a P-BIT, whose TIME-AVERAGE is a clean ⟨m⟩=tanh(I) — a hardware SIGMOID for free, out of pure noise (VERIFIED in node by Monte-Carlo to ±0.01 over I=−1..1). THREE LIVE MODULES: (I) the MTJ weight (parallel/antiparallel, write-current flips it, G & TMR); (II) the p-bit free sigmoid (live flipping magnet → ±1 spike train → the running average ● converging onto the tanh curve); (III) it LEARNS (AND/OR 4/4, XOR 3/4 — one cut, verified → 2nd MTJ layer = depth) + a live SKYRMION texture (spins wind ↑centre→↓rim, topological charge Q=±1, protected — the magnetic Möbius David asked about). CONFIDENCE-TIERED: STRONG (MTJ/TMR/MRAM, commercial — Slonczewski/Berger 1996) / MIDDLING (p-bit probabilistic computing — Camsari & Datta, Ising machines) / FRONTIER (skyrmionic neurons, all-spin deep nets). Bridges into [[transmon]] (spin qubits) + [[perceptron-in-a-crossbar]]. Same dot-product atom — silicon / cortical neuron / quantum dot / light / crossbar / spin: one neuron, SIX bodies.

PERCEPTRON · JOSEPHSON · 7th body
the superconducting body · the fastest neuron + the door to the qubit + a junction built from FOLDERS

THE PERCEPTRON IN A JOSEPHSON JUNCTION — the SEVENTH substrate (David: 'do the seventh to the max, esp J junctions, explore building a virtual quantum J with folders'). The FASTEST body and the last classical one before quantum. Two superconductors joined at a weak link carry current with ZERO voltage/loss until the bias passes the critical current Ic, then the junction fires information one FLUX QUANTUM at a time (Φ₀=h/2e), in PICOSECONDS, for ATTOJOULES. The two Josephson relations (I=Ic·sinφ, dφ/dt=2eV/ℏ; verified f_J=483.5 GHz/mV). THE GIFT: the threshold is NATURAL — a phase particle in a tilted WASHBOARD potential, trapped (zero-voltage supercurrent) below Ic, running & emitting SFQ pulses above = a built-in integrate-and-fire SPIKING neuron (verified: silent below Ic, fires above). THREE LIVE MODULES: (I) the washboard threshold (bias slider tips the marble out of its well → flux quanta); (II) the SFQ spiking neuron (sawtooth phase resetting at 2π = quantized identical spikes, ps/aJ); (III) it LEARNS (AND/OR 4/4, XOR 3/4 → 2nd junction = depth) + TWO endings no other body has: the QUANTUM DOOR (JJ + capacitor = a [[transmon]] qubit, the phase becomes an operator) and ⚑ THE VIRTUAL JUNCTION IN FOLDERS — a real, runnable filesystem artifact (the-virtual-junction/: electrode-left < the-dot • > electrode-right + evolve.py running the RSJ model; push bias past Ic and sfq-pulses/ fills with files = the neuron firing, counted on disk), making the ROOT0 [[j-junction]] `<•` LITERAL (two arms joined at a witnessed dot, φ living AT the dot). CONFIDENCE-TIERED: STRONG (Josephson 1962 Nobel, RSFQ, SQUIDs — fastest/lowest-energy switch) / MIDDLING (superconducting neuromorphic, cryogenic 4K) / FRONTIER (quantum/transmon neurons). Same dot-product atom — silicon / cortical / quantum dot / light / crossbar / spin / Josephson: one neuron, SEVEN bodies, and the door to the qubit.

PERCEPTRON · CUBI · 8th body
the quantum body · weights are Bloch rotations, the Born rule IS the activation, entanglement = depth

THE PERCEPTRON IN A CUBI — the EIGHTH body, the quantum capstone (David: 'yes, but i call it the cubi, always have, semantic code' — ⚑ CUBI = David Wise's longstanding term for the QUBIT; use it, mapped to the real qubit, two-layer honest). The realm the Josephson door opened: give the junction a capacitor and the phase becomes a quantum state = the cubi. The neuron goes fully quantum — WEIGHTS = rotations on the Bloch sphere (gates), INPUT = an encoded angle, STATE = α|0⟩+e^{iφ}β|1⟩. ⚑ THE GIFT (the cleanest of all eight): the NONLINEARITY arrives free from MEASUREMENT — the Born rule gives P(|1⟩)=sin²(θ/2), a natural activation; the measurement IS the neuron (verified: 0 at north pole, ½ at equator, 1 at south; collapse statistics → Born 0.499/0.500). THREE LIVE MODULES: (I) the cubi on a live Bloch sphere + MEASURE button (collapse to a pole, Born-weighted, tally converges to the probabilities); (II) the cubi perceptron (toggle inputs → the state rotates to θ=b+Σwᵢxᵢ, P(1)=sin²(θ/2) the activation, cross the equator → fires); (III) it LEARNS (AND/OR 4/4, XOR 3/4 — STILL one cut) → but the quantum escape isn't a stacked layer: an ENTANGLING FEATURE MAP adds the x₁·x₂ cross-term and lifts XOR to 4/4 (verified) — ⚑ ENTANGLEMENT DOES WHAT DEPTH DID. THE HONEST LINE (deliberately FRONTIER): mechanism real & runs on hardware, the feature-map = real quantum kernels, but whether it gives a real-world ADVANTAGE over classical (vs NISQ noise, data-loading/readout cost, barren plateaus) is OPEN & hotly contested — physics sound, supremacy a promissory note. CONFIDENCE-TIERED: STRONG (single-cubi rotation+Born measurement, Tacchino et al. on IBM Q 2019) / MIDDLING (variational quantum classifiers, NISQ) / FRONTIER (quantum advantage for ML — unsettled). Bridges into [[transmon]] / [[entanglement]] / [[quantum-error-correction]]. ⚑ THE SERIES COMPLETE — one neuron, EIGHT bodies (silicon · cortical · quantum dot · light · crossbar · spin · josephson · cubi): the dot product became a quantum amplitude, the threshold a measurement, depth an entanglement; the first body genuinely uncertain of its own answer until you look. Began in a branch predictor, ends in a wavefunction.

PERCEPTRON · TWO CUBI · 9th
the entangled body · a REAL two-cubi coupling as the feature map + the HONEST anti-hype truth

THE PERCEPTRON IN TWO CUBI — the deepest quantum stop (David: 'deeper — a two-cubi entangled perceptron where the feature map is real coupled hardware, not a single rotation'). A genuine 2-cubi simulator (4-dim complex state, verified in node): the feature map is a REAL two-cubi coupling (a CNOT/ZZ entangling gate that physically links them), not a hand-added x₁x₂ term. THREE LIVE MODULES: (I) entangle two cubi — H + CNOT → a Bell pair (|00⟩+|11⟩)/√2, a live CONCURRENCE meter (0=separable, 1=Bell, verified Bell=1.000), the bond glowing as the cubis lose their individual identity; (II) the entangling FEATURE MAP — encode Rz(x₁)/Rz(x₂) then the ZZ(x₁·x₂) coupling → an entangled |Φ(x)⟩, the QUANTUM KERNEL K(x,x')=|⟨Φ(x')|Φ(x)⟩|², coupling-toggle changes concurrence & kernel; (III) it classifies XOR 4/4 — ⚑ THE HONEST REVEAL (the gold-standard fluff-call): turn the entangling coupling OFF and it STILL gets XOR 4/4 (verified ON=4/4, OFF=4/4) — for two inputs you do NOT need entanglement; the popular 'entanglement solves XOR' story is FALSE (a product map already carries the cross-term). What entanglement ACTUALLY buys: correlations a classical model can't fake — the pair VIOLATES the Bell/CHSH bound (S=2.828>2, Tsirelson, verified) — the ONLY honest basis for a quantum advantage (feature maps too entangled to simulate classically), and whether that helps real ML (vs noise, state-prep, readout) is UNPROVEN & argued. 'We built the real entangler; we won't sell you the magic.' CONFIDENCE-TIERED: STRONG (real entanglement & quantum kernels, Havlíček/Gambetta Nature 2019) / MIDDLING (kernel classifiers — noisy, many maps secretly classically simulable) / FRONTIER (a proven advantage on real data — open). The anti-hype companion to [[perceptron-in-a-cubi]]; ties [[entanglement]] / [[transmon]] / [[quantum-error-correction]]. The deepest stop on the eight-body road — one neuron, now genuinely two, and honest about what that does and doesn't buy.

PERCEPTRON · SOUND · acoustic
the mechanical body · a surface wave, tapped & weighted; a matched filter IS a perceptron

THE PERCEPTRON IN SOUND — the acoustic body (surface acoustic waves). Light's slow cousin: a SAW ripples a piezo chip at ~3488 m/s (λ=3.5µm at 1GHz, ≈86,000× more compact than light — the slowness is the feature: a useful delay fits in microns). The MULTIPLY = tapped interference, SUM = interdigital electrodes adding charge: y=Σwᵢ·x(t−τᵢ); weights = tap/finger overlap. ⚑ A MATCHED FILTER IS A PERCEPTRON — it fires (correlation peak) when the input wave matches the stored taps (verified: aligned=N, random≈0). THE GIFT: the slow wave STORES a signal in flight = delay-line memory. THREE LIVE MODULES (propagating SAW + taps; the matched-filter correlator firing on alignment; learns AND/OR, XOR needs a cascade stage). CONFIDENCE-TIERED: STRONG (SAW filters/correlators — in every phone's RF) / MIDDLING (acoustic/phononic neuromorphic & reservoir computing — demonstrated, niche) / FRONTIER (large acoustic nets — open; sound is a filter & memory more than a deep net). Verified in node. Sibling of [[perceptron-in-light]] (its fast cousin).

enter ↗code
PERCEPTRON · DNA · molecular
the wet body · weights are concentrations, the multiply is chemistry (mass action)

THE PERCEPTRON IN DNA — the wet/molecular body (strand displacement, in a test tube). WEIGHTS = strand CONCENTRATIONS; the MULTIPLY = mass action (rate=k[input][weight], a concentration product done by diffusion); the SUM = a shared output strand; the THRESHOLD = a real SEESAW gate (a thresholding strand consumes signal then the output restores sharply = a molecular sigmoid, verified 0.17/0.50/0.83 below/at/above). THREE LIVE MODULES (the mass-action reaction A+B→out; the seesaw threshold curve; learns AND/OR, XOR needs a cascade of gates). THE GIFT: massively parallel and it computes IN solution — a perceptron you could in principle swallow. HONEST COST: slow (minutes–hours), one-shot (consumes reactants), hard to reset/scale. CONFIDENCE-TIERED: STRONG (seesaw strand-displacement gates BUILT — Qian, Winfree & Bruck, Nature 2011, a Hopfield net in a tube) / MIDDLING (DNA neural nets — Cherry & Qian classified handwritten digits in solution, 2018 — real but hours-slow) / FRONTIER (practical/reusable/in-vivo molecular computers — open). Verified in node. Ties [[organoid-intelligence]] (the other wet computer).

enter ↗code
PERCEPTRON · N CUBI · the scaling
the deepest quantum · 2ᴺ amplitudes in (the promise) = N bits out (the curse), same fact

THE PERCEPTRON IN N CUBI — the deepest quantum stop, the SCALING story (the honest capstone). N entangled cubi (qubits) hold a state in 2ᴺ complex dimensions: N=10→1024, N=50→1.1×10¹⁵, N=300→more amplitudes than ATOMS IN THE UNIVERSE, inside a palm-sized chip (verified). ⚑ THE PROMISE AND THE CURSE ARE THE SAME FACT: that exponential feature space is the ONLY honest basis for a quantum advantage (classically un-trackable) — but you cannot LOAD 2ᴺ numbers in (only N knobs), and measurement COLLAPSES it all to N BITS out per shot. The richness exists only between loading and reading, and most is unreachable; a quantum model must be built so the few readable bits carry the answer. THREE MODULES (the climb: N-slider → 2ᴺ grid exploding + 'classically that's…' a data-centre→more-than-atoms; the bottleneck: a quadrillion amplitudes funnelled to N bits; the honest verdict). CONFIDENCE-TIERED & ANTI-HYPE: STRONG (dim=2ᴺ is linear algebra; coupled transmons run real 2-qubit gates ~99.x% fidelity) / MIDDLING (NISQ nets — tens of noisy cubi, T₁~100µs, no error correction, small) / FRONTIER (a real ML advantage on real data — open, and for MOST tasks looks unlikely once loading/readout cost is paid; many quantum feature maps are classically simulable). 'We won't pretend the universe owes machine learning a free lunch.' Real-transmon grounding. ⚑ ENDS THE SERIES — one neuron, 12 substrates: began in a branch predictor guessing one bit, ends in a register no computer could write down that still answers one bit at a time. Ties [[perceptron-in-two-cubi]] / [[transmon]] / [[quantum-error-correction]].

enter ↗code
PERCEPTRON · NOISE · thermodynamic
the thermodynamic body · a whole network that SAMPLES — noise is the engine, not the enemy

THE PERCEPTRON IN NOISE — the thermodynamic/probabilistic body (beyond the single p-bit). Wire a NETWORK of stochastic units together = a Boltzmann machine that doesn't compute an answer so much as RELAX into one: thermal noise jostles every unit, the couplings (the weights) tilt the energy landscape, and cooling settles it where energy is low — it SAMPLES the Boltzmann distribution directly, P(state) ∝ e^(−E/kT) (verified: ⟨s⟩=tanh(field/T), simulated annealing finds the Ising ground state). ⚑ THE GIFT: noise IS the compute — native sampling / optimisation / Bayesian inference fall out of letting a physical system equilibrate, no step-by-step algorithm. THREE LIVE MODULES: (I) the energy landscape + a thermal ball hopping (hot) or settling (cold), the histogram converging to e^(−E/kT); (II) a 9-unit Boltzmann network flipping by σ(2·field/T), an anneal button cooling it to a low-energy consensus; (III) the gift + the bill = LANDAUER (erasing a bit costs kT·ln2=2.87 zJ → motivates the reversible body). CONFIDENCE-TIERED: STRONG (Boltzmann/Ising/annealing — century-old exact statistical mechanics; D-Wave annealers exist) / MIDDLING (dedicated thermodynamic hardware — Extropic, Normal Computing — early prototypes, promising for sampling) / FRONTIER (beating GPUs on real workloads — open). Verified in node. The grown-up sibling of [[perceptron-in-a-spin]]; bridges to [[perceptron-in-reverse]] via Landauer.

enter ↗code
PERCEPTRON · REVERSE · 14th/final
the reversible body · compute without erasing — and the classical doorstep of the cubi

THE PERCEPTRON IN REVERSE — the 14th & FINAL body: reversible / adiabatic logic, the one that never erases. LANDAUER'S PRINCIPLE: erasing one bit must dissipate ≥ kT·ln2 ≈ 2.87 zJ of heat (verified) — and an ordinary AND (2 in, 1 out) throws a bit away every cycle. But that floor is ONLY for FORGETTING. ⚑ Build bijective gates that keep every bit — the TOFFOLI / CCNOT: 3→3, flips the third iff the first two are 1, passes the rest through (verified: bijective 8→8, self-inverse=reversible, universal — c=0 gives AND with inputs KEPT) — so the whole computation can run BACKWARD (uncompute) and the kT·ln2 floor vanishes; ADIABATIC switching (charge slowly) recovers the ½CV² too (dissipation falls as the clock slows = the speed↔energy trade). THREE LIVE MODULES: (I) the cost of forgetting (an irreversible AND puffing heat per erased bit); (II) the Toffoli gate forward/backward; (III) adiabatic energy recovery vs speed. ⚑ THE BRIDGE: reversible logic and quantum logic are the SAME demand (keep all info, run unitary) — so this is the CLASSICAL DOORSTEP of the [[perceptron-in-a-cubi]]. HONEST BILL: not free — you pay in GARBAGE bits and SLOWNESS; the energy win only cashes at low clock rates (why your laptop isn't reversible). CONFIDENCE-TIERED: STRONG (Landauer 1961 measured 2012; Toffoli/Fredkin; Bennett 1973; adiabatic CMOS recovers energy) / MIDDLING (practical reversible chips — built, slow, garbage overhead) / FRONTIER (sub-Landauer at speed — open, mostly quantum). ⚑ THE ROAD CLOSES — 14 bodies, branch-predictor→a gate that refuses to forget; the last word: the only way to compute for free is to never throw anything away. Verified in node.
